                    <TableRow>
                      <TableCell colSpan={3} className="text-center text-muted-foreground py-6 whitespace-nowrap">No recent enquiries</TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ) : (
                    recentEnquiries.map((enq: any) => (
                      <TableRow key={enq.id}>
                        <TableCell className="whitespace-nowrap font-medium">{(enq.payload as any)?.name || (enq.payload as any)?.email || 'Unknown'}</TableCell>

                    ))
                  )}

            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>

                  {recentBlogs.length === 0 ? (

                    </TableRow>
                  ) : (

      </div>
    </div>
  );

with open('/workspace/app-d6a1n87ee60x/src/pages/content_new/Products.tsx', 'r') as f:

import re

# 1. Fetch categories
content = content.replace("const [dataList, setDataList] = useState<any[]>([]);", "const [dataList, setDataList] = useState<any[]>([]);\n  const [categories, setCategories] = useState<any[]>([]);")

fetch_pattern = re.compile(r"const fetchData = async \(\) => \{.*?setDataList\(data \|\| \[\]\);.*?\}", re.DOTALL)
new_fetch = """const fetchData = async () => {
      setLoading(true);
      try {
        const { data } = await supabase.from('products').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false });
        setDataList(data || []);
        const { data: catData } = await supabase.from('product_categories').select('id, name');
        setCategories(catData || []);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(err);
        toast.error('Failed to load data');
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    }"""
content = fetch_pattern.sub(new_fetch, content)

# 2. Add Select import
if "from '@/components/ui/select'" not in content:
    content = content.replace("import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';", "import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';\nimport { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from '@/components/ui/select';")

# 3. Replace category_id input with Select
old_cat_input = r"""<FormField control=\{form\.control\} name="category_id" render=\{\(\{ field \}\) => \(
                  <FormItem>
                    <FormLabel>Category Id</FormLabel>
                    <FormControl><Input \{\.\.\.field\} value=\{field\.value \|\| ''\} /></FormControl>
                    <FormMessage />
                  </FormItem>
                \)\} />"""
new_cat_input = """<FormField control={form.control} name="category_id" render={({ field }) => (
                  <FormItem>
                    <FormLabel>Category</FormLabel>
                    <Select value={field.value || ''} onValueChange={field.onChange}>
                      <FormControl>
                        <SelectTrigger>
                          <SelectValue placeholder="Select Category" />
                        </SelectTrigger>
                      </FormControl>
                      <SelectContent>
                        {categories.map((c) => (
                          <SelectItem key={c.id} value={c.id}>{c.name}</SelectItem>
                        ))}
                      </SelectContent>
                    </Select>
                    <FormMessage />
                  </FormItem>
                )} />"""
content = re.sub(old_cat_input, new_cat_input, content)

# 4. Also auto-generate slug from name
old_name_input = r"""<FormField control=\{form\.control\} name="name" render=\{\(\{ field \}\) => \(
                  <FormItem>
                    <FormLabel>Name</FormLabel>
                    <FormControl><Input \{\.\.\.field\} value=\{field\.value \|\| ''\} /></FormControl>
                    <FormMessage />
                  </FormItem>
                \)\} />"""
new_name_input = """<FormField control={form.control} name="name" render={({ field }) => (
                  <FormItem>
                    <FormLabel>Name</FormLabel>
                    <FormControl><Input {...field} value={field.value || ''} onChange={(e) => {
                      field.onChange(e);
                      const generatedSlug = e.target.value.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/(^-|-$)+/g, '');
                      form.setValue('slug', generatedSlug);
                    }} /></FormControl>
                    <FormMessage />
                  </FormItem>
                )} />"""
content = re.sub(old_name_input, new_name_input, content)

# 5. Table headers - Change "Category Id" to "Category"
content = content.replace("<TableHead>Category Id</TableHead>", "<TableHead>Category</TableHead>")
content = content.replace('<TableCell className="max-w-xs truncate">{item.category_id}</TableCell>', '<TableCell className="max-w-xs truncate">{categories.find(c => c.id === item.category_id)?.name || item.category_id}</TableCell>')

with open('/workspace/app-d6a1n87ee60x/src/pages/content_new/Products.tsx', 'w') as f:
    f.write(content)
EOF
python3 /workspace/app-d6a1n87ee60x/tasks/fix_products.py

import re

with open('/workspace/app-d6a1n87ee60x/src/pages/content_new/GlobalPartners.tsx', 'r') as f:
    content = f.read()

# Replace Zod schema
old_schema = r"""const partnerSchema = z\.object\(\{
  name: z\.string\(\)\.min\(1, 'Name is required'\),
  logo: z\.string\(\)\.min\(1, 'Logo is required'\),
  website_url: z\.string\(\)\.optional\(\),
  partner_type: z\.string\(\),
  display_order: z\.coerce\.number\(\),
  is_active: z\.boolean\(\),
\}\);"""

new_schema = """const partnerSchema = z.object({
  name: z.string().min(1, 'Name is required'),
  image_url: z.string().min(1, 'Image is required'),
  order_index: z.coerce.number(),
});"""

content = re.sub(old_schema, new_schema, content)

# Default values
content = content.replace("defaultValues: { name: '', logo: '', website_url: '', partner_type: 'Domestic', display_order: 0, is_active: true }", "defaultValues: { name: '', image_url: '', order_index: 0 }")

# Fetch ordering
content = content.replace("order('display_order', { ascending: true })", "order('order_index', { ascending: true })")

# Table Headers
old_th = r"""<TableHead>Logo</TableHead>
                  <TableHead>Name</TableHead>
                  <TableHead>Type</TableHead>
                  <TableHead>Status</TableHead>"""
new_th = """<TableHead>Image</TableHead>
                  <TableHead>Name</TableHead>
                  <TableHead>Order</TableHead>"""
content = re.sub(old_th, new_th, content)

# Table Cells
old_tc = r"""<TableCell>
                        <img src=\{item\.logo\} alt=\{item\.name\} className="h-10 object-contain" />
                      </TableCell>
                      <TableCell className="font-medium">\{item\.name\}</TableCell>
                      <TableCell>\{item\.partner_type\}</TableCell>
                      <TableCell>
                        <Badge variant=\{item\.is_active \? 'default' : 'secondary'\}>
                          \{item\.is_active \? 'Active' : 'Inactive'\}
                        </Badge>
                      </TableCell>"""
new_tc = """<TableCell>
                        <img src={item.image_url} alt={item.name} className="h-10 object-contain" />
                      </TableCell>
                      <TableCell className="font-medium">{item.name}</TableCell>
                      <TableCell>{item.order_index}</TableCell>"""
content = re.sub(old_tc, new_tc, content)

# Form Fields (Logo -> Image URL)
old_logo_field = r"""<FormField control=\{form\.control\} name="logo" render=\{\(\{ field \}\) => \(
                  <FormItem>
                    <FormLabel>Logo URL</FormLabel>
                    <FormControl><Input \{\.\.\.field\} /></FormControl>
                    <FormMessage />
                  </FormItem>
                \)\} />"""
new_logo_field = """<FormField control={form.control} name="image_url" render={({ field }) => (
                  <FormItem>
                    <FormLabel>Image URL</FormLabel>
                    <FormControl><Input {...field} /></FormControl>
                    <FormMessage />
                  </FormItem>
                )} />"""
content = re.sub(old_logo_field, new_logo_field, content)

# Form Fields (display_order -> order_index)
old_order_field = r"""<FormField control=\{form\.control\} name="display_order" render=\{\(\{ field \}\) => \(
                  <FormItem>
                    <FormLabel>Display Order</FormLabel>
                    <FormControl><Input type="number" \{\.\.\.field\} /></FormControl>
                    <FormMessage />
                  </FormItem>
                \)\} />"""
new_order_field = """<FormField control={form.control} name="order_index" render={({ field }) => (
                  <FormItem>
                    <FormLabel>Order Index</FormLabel>
                    <FormControl><Input type="number" {...field} /></FormControl>
                    <FormMessage />
                  </FormItem>
                )} />"""
content = re.sub(old_order_field, new_order_field, content)

# Remove unused fields
content = re.sub(r'<FormField control=\{form\.control\} name="website_url".*?</FormItem>\s*\)} />', '', content, flags=re.DOTALL)
content = re.sub(r'<FormField control=\{form\.control\} name="partner_type".*?</FormItem>\s*\)} />', '', content, flags=re.DOTALL)
content = re.sub(r'<FormField control=\{form\.control\} name="is_active".*?</FormItem>\s*\)} />', '', content, flags=re.DOTALL)

with open('/workspace/app-d6a1n87ee60x/src/pages/content_new/GlobalPartners.tsx', 'w') as f:
    f.write(content)
EOF

const { createClient } = require('@supabase/supabase-js');
require('dotenv').config({ path: '/workspace/app-d6a1n87ee60x/.env' });

const supabaseUrl = process.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL;
const supabaseKey = process.env.VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY;
const supabase = createClient(supabaseUrl, supabaseKey);

async function run() {
    const sql = `
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS global_partners (
      id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
      name TEXT NOT NULL,
      image_url TEXT,
      order_index INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
      created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT NOW()
    );

    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tool_room_machines (
      id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
      name TEXT NOT NULL,
      specs TEXT NOT NULL,
      type TEXT NOT NULL,
      image_url TEXT,
      is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
      order_index INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
      created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT NOW()
    );

    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS vision_mission (
      id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
      title TEXT NOT NULL,
      description TEXT NOT NULL,
      icon_name TEXT NOT NULL,
      order_index INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
      created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT NOW()
    );

    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS leadership_team (
      id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
      name TEXT NOT NULL,
      role TEXT NOT NULL,
      order_index INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
      created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT NOW()
    );

    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS operations_team (
      id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
      name TEXT NOT NULL,
      role TEXT NOT NULL,
      order_index INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
      created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT NOW()
    );
    `;
    const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke('run-sql', {
        body: { query: sql }
    });
    console.log(error || data);
}
run();
EOF

import re

with open('/workspace/app-d6a1n87ee60x/src/pages/content_new/GlobalPartners.tsx', 'r') as f:

# Fix form.reset for ADD
old_reset_add = r"""form\.reset\(\{ name: '', logo: '', website_url: '', partner_type: 'Domestic', display_order: 0, is_active: true \}\);"""
new_reset_add = """form.reset({ name: '', image_url: '', order_index: 0 });"""

# Fix form.reset for EDIT
old_reset_edit = r"""form\.reset\(\{ name: item\.name, logo: item\.logo, website_url: item\.website_url, partner_type: item\.partner_type, display_order: item\.display_order, is_active: item\.is_active \}\);"""
new_reset_edit = """form.reset({ name: item.name, image_url: item.image_url, order_index: item.order_index });"""
content = re.sub(old_reset_edit, new_reset_edit, content)

# Fix the file upload setValue
content = content.replace("form.setValue('logo', url)", "form.setValue('image_url', url)")
content = content.replace("form.watch('logo')", "form.watch('image_url')")

with open('/workspace/app-d6a1n87ee60x/src/pages/content_new/GlobalPartners.tsx', 'w') as f:
    f.write(content)

cat << 'EOF' > /workspace/app-d6a1n87ee60x/tasks/fix_global_partners3.py
with open('/workspace/app-d6a1n87ee60x/src/pages/content_new/GlobalPartners.tsx', 'r') as f:
    content = f.read()

import re

# Fix handleEdit form.reset multiline
old_multiline_reset = r"""    form\.reset\(\{ 
      name: partner\.name, 
      logo: partner\.logo \|\| '', 
      website_url: partner\.website_url \|\| '', 
      partner_type: partner\.partner_type \|\| 'Domestic',
      display_order: partner\.display_order,
      is_active: partner\.is_active
    \}\);"""
new_multiline_reset = """    form.reset({ 
      name: partner.name, 
      image_url: partner.image_url || '', 
      order_index: partner.order_index || 0
    });"""
content = re.sub(old_multiline_reset, new_multiline_reset, content)

# Fix FileUpload field
old_logo_field = r"""<FormField control=\{form\.control\} name="logo" render=\{\(\{ field \}\) => \(
                  <FormItem><FormLabel>Logo Image \*</FormLabel><FormControl><FileUpload value=\{field\.value\} onChange=\{field\.onChange\} /></FormControl><FormMessage /></FormItem>
                \)\} />"""
new_logo_field = """<FormField control={form.control} name="image_url" render={({ field }) => (
                  <FormItem><FormLabel>Image *</FormLabel><FormControl><FileUpload value={field.value || ''} onChange={field.onChange} /></FormControl><FormMessage /></FormItem>
                )} />"""
content = re.sub(old_logo_field, new_logo_field, content)

# Fix display_order field
old_order_field = r"""<FormField control=\{form\.control\} name="display_order" render=\{\(\{ field \}\) => \(
                    <FormItem><FormLabel>Display Order</FormLabel><FormControl><Input type="number" \{\.\.\.field\} /></FormControl><FormMessage /></FormItem>
                  \)\} />"""
new_order_field = """<FormField control={form.control} name="order_index" render={({ field }) => (
                    <FormItem><FormLabel>Order</FormLabel><FormControl><Input type="number" {...field} onChange={e => field.onChange(parseInt(e.target.value) || 0)} /></FormControl><FormMessage /></FormItem>
                  )} />"""
content = re.sub(old_order_field, new_order_field, content)

with open('/workspace/app-d6a1n87ee60x/src/pages/content_new/GlobalPartners.tsx', 'w') as f:
    f.write(content)
EOF
